{D148}]@##/_rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya ga ga na gany+dza pa ri pr-its+tsha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa nam mkha' mdzod kyis zhus pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la 
[\[243a.2\]]phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul rin po che bkod pa'i 'khor gyi khyams de bzhin gshegs pa'i gnas/_bsod nams chen po'i rgyan gyis tshogs bsags pa/_spyod pa chen po'i rgyu 'thun 
[\[243a.3\]]pa/_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad rnam par smin pas mngon par grub pa/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i gnas mtha' yas pa nges par bstan pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par sprul pa'i byin gyi rlabs/_mi chags pa'i spyod yul gyi ye shes 
[\[243a.4\]]la 'jug pa/_dga' ba rgya chen po rab tu skyed pa/_dran pa dang /_blo gros dang /_rtogs pa la 'jug pa/_kun nas ma smad pa/_ye shes kyis rnam par dpyod pa/_de'i rang bzhin ma yin pa la 'jug pa/_phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal par mngon du bstod pa/_yon tan gyi tshogs 
[\[243a.5\]]thams cad dpag tu med pa na bzhugs te/_bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid ni mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas/_chos kyi 'khor lo ni rab tu bskor/_slob ma shin tu dul ba mtha' yas pa'i tshogs ni mnga'/_chos thams cad la ni mnga' brnyes/_sems 
[\[243a.6\]]can thams cad kyi bsam pa'i bya ba ni shin tu mkhyen/_dbang po dam pa'i pha rol du ni byon/_sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i bag chags kyis mtshams sbyor ba yang dag par 'joms pa la ni mkhas shing /_sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa lhun gyis grub pa ni rgyun ma bcad do/_/
[\[243a.7\]]dge slong sa ya phrag drug gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs cig ste/_thams cad kyang cang shes kyi sems dang ldan pa/_yang dag pa'i shes pas sems shin tu rnam par grol ba/_nyon mongs pa thams cad kyi bag chags kyis mtshams sbyor ba yang dag par 'joms

[\[243b.1\]]pa la brtson pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi rgyal po'i sras chos zab mo la gnas par mkhas pa/_dmigs su med pa'i chos las nges par byung ba/_spyod lam mdzes pa phun sum tshogs pa/_sbyin pa'i gnas chen por gyur pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' la shin 
[\[243b.2\]]tu zhugs pa sha stag go/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing sna tshogs nas 'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dpag tu med grangs med bsam gyis mi khyab mtshungs pa med pa gzhal du med pa tshad med pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs chen 
[\[243b.3\]]po dang yang thabs cig ste/_thams cad kyang sems kyi skad cig gis sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' yas par 'gro ba la mkhas pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la mchod pa dang /_rim gro bya ba la mkhas pa/_chos nyan pa la mi ngoms par yang dag par tshol ba/_rtag tu 
[\[243b.4\]]rgyun mi chad par sems can thams cad yongs su smin par byed pa la brtson pa/_thabs dang shes rab dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa/_sgrib pa med pa'i rnam par thar pa la rab tu gnas pa/_rtog pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa dang /_spros pa thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa/_
[\[243b.5\]]thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la nye bar gyur pa sha stag la 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' glog gi lha zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' g.yul las rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnam par snang mdzad snying po zhes bya ba dang /_byang 
[\[243b.6\]]chub sems dpa' rnam par gnon pa dpa' bo zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' yid gnyis yang dag par 'joms pa zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnam par bsgrags ldan zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnam par lta ba'i mig ces bya ba dang /_byang chub

[\[244b.1\]]'khor rnams kyang mi snang ste/_gzhan du na nam mkha'i dkyil na gnas pa'i khang pa brtsegs pa de dag nyid du shes shing /_bcom ldan 'das kyi seng ge'i khri ni 'phang du dpag tshad khrir 'phags par rab tu snang ngo /_/gang la bcom ldan 'das bzhugs pa'i seng ge'i

[\[262b.1\]]'di shes so/_/rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bsod nams nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa gang zhe na/_rigs kyi bu sangs rgyas kyi chos ni nam mkha' dang mtshungs te/_de dag gi byang chub kyi sems kyi sa bon de yang nam mkha' dang mtshungs so/_/rigs 
[\[262b.2\]]kyi bu byang chub sems dpa'i bsod nams ji snyed mngon par 'du bya ba mngon par 'du byed pa de yang byang chub kyi sems dang mi 'bral te dge ba'i rtsa ba bsags shing bsags pa de dag kyang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du sngo bar byed cing de dag thams cad kyang nam mkha' dang mtshungs par 
[\[262b.3\]]tshad med par rab tu gnas so/_/de la byang chub sems dpas 'di ltar nam mkha' tshad med pa'i phyir bdag gis bsod nams tshad med pa mngon par 'du bya'o snyam du sems bskyed par bya'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_tshad med par brtsams pas tshad med pa'i don 'phel ba yin 
[\[262b.4\]]te/_de la byang chub sems dpas bsod nams kyi tshogs tshad med pa la brtson par bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu tshad med pa bcu po 'di dag ni gang byang chub sems dpas bsod nams kyi tshogs kyis yongs su rdzogs par bya ba ste/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_mtshan dang dpe 
[\[262b.5\]]byad bzang po yongs su rdzogs pas lus kyi rgyan tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_sems can thams cad kyi rjes su song ba'i dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag pas ngag gi rgyan tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_sems can thams cad kyi 
[\[262b.6\]]sems kyi rjes su 'jug pas sems kyi rgyan tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_spyod pa dang ye shes dang spyod lam tshad med pas sems can yongs su smin par bya ba tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_rnam pa tshad med pa rnams kyis sangs rgyas kyi 
[\[262b.7\]]zhing yongs su dag pa tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_bsod nams dang sems nges par rtog pa dang dpyod pa tshad med pas sangs rgyas kyi spyod lam tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_rgyan bkod pa thams cad dang spyod pa thams cad kyis byang chub 
[\[263a\]]
[\[263a.1\]]kyi snying po'i rgyan tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_gtan pa med pa'i mchod sbyin yongs su rdzogs pas mdzod spu yongs su spyad pa tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_bla ma la rim gro byed cing 'dud pa tshad med pa dang nga rgyal bcom pa tshad 
[\[263a.2\]]med pas dbu'i gtsug tor spyi gtsug bltar mi mthong ba tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_sgyu dang g.yo med par 'ong ba dang 'gro ba dang 'thun par 'dzin pas don yod par rnam par gnon pa tshad med pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba ste/_rigs kyi bu tshad med pa bcu po de 
[\[263a.3\]]dag ni gang byang chub sems dpas bsod nams kyi tshogs kyis yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'o/_/gzhan yang rigs kyi bu nam byang chub sems dpa' sems nam mkha' dang mtshungs par gyur pa de nam mkha' tshad med pas de'i rtsom pa thams cad kyang tshad med par kun du gnas 
[\[263a.4\]]te/_de bas na bsod nams nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa zhes bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' ye shes nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa yin zhe na/_gang sems can thams cad kyi sems la ye shes des khyab par byed cing /_sems can thams cad kyi sems 
[\[263a.5\]]'dod chags dang bcas pa'i sems kyang 'dod chags dang bcas pa'i sems su yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes so/_/zhe sdang dang bcas pa'i sems kyang zhe sdang dang bcas pa'i sems su yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes so/_/gti mug dang bcas pa'i 
[\[263a.6\]]sems kyang gti mug dang bcas pa'i sems su yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes so/_/nye ba'i nyon mongs pa dang bcas pa'i sems kyang des nye ba'i nyon mongs pa dang bcas pa'i sems su yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes so/_/des bdag 'dod chags 
[\[263a.7\]]med pa'i chos kyi rang bzhin las ma g.yos par sems can gzhan rnams dang gang zag gzhan dag la 'dod chags 'dul ba'i phyir chos 'chad do/_/des bdag nyid zhe sdang med pa'i chos kyi rang bzhin las ma g.yos par sems can gzhan rnams dang gang zag gzhan dag la 
[\[263b\]]
[\[263b.1\]]zhe sdang 'dul ba'i phyir chos 'chad do/_/des bdag nyid gti mug med pa'i chos kyi rang bzhin las ma g.yos par sems can gzhan rnams dang gang zag gzhan dag la gti mug 'dul ba'i phyir chos 'chad do/_/des bdag nyid nye ba'i nyon mongs pa med pa'i chos kyi rang bzhin 
[\[263b.2\]]las ma g.yos par sems can gzhan rnams dang /_gang zag gzhan dag la nyon mongs pa thams cad 'dul ba'i phyir chos 'chad do/_/des 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi nyon mongs pa rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir chos 'chad do/_/'dod chags dang /_
[\[263b.3\]]zhe sdang dang /_gti mug dang nye ba'i nyon mongs pa'i sems la dman par yang yang dag par rjes su mi mthong /_'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug dang nye ba'i nyon mongs pa med pa'i sems la mchog tu yang yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ngo /_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di 
[\[263b.4\]]ltar byang chub sems dpa' de gnyis su med pa'i mtha'i sgo nas chos kyi dbyings yongs su shes te/_chos kyi dbyings gang yin pa de 'dod chags kyi dbyings/_chos kyi dbyings gang yin pa de zhe sdang gi dbyings/_chos kyi dbyings gang yin pa de gti mug gi dbyings/_chos kyi 
[\[263b.5\]]dbyings gang yin pa de nyon mongs pa'i dbyings so/_/de bas na chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings su yang dag par 'du'o zhes bya ste/_chos kyi dbyings ni gar yang 'gro ba'ang med/_mi 'gro ba'ang med do/_/chos thams cad ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rjes su song ste/_gang bdag 
[\[263b.6\]]gi dbyings shes pa des chos kyi dbyings kyang shes so/_/de ltar na chos kyi dbyings dang bdag gi dbyings de dag mi gnyis so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_bdag rnam par dag pas chos kyi dbyings kyang rnam par dag go/_/de bzhin du chos thams cad kyang rnam par dag pa ste/_
[\[263b.7\]]gang rnam par dag pa de rab tu snang ba'o/_/gang rab tu snang ba de bla gab med pa'o/_/gang bla gab med pa de la mtshan ma thams cad kyi skabs med do/_/gang mtshan ma med pa de la gnas pa gang yang med de/_mi gnas pa ni nam mkha'o/_/gang de ltar ye shes kyi rjes su 
[\[264a\]]
[\[264a.1\]]'gro zhing ye shes des chos thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro ba de ni byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa gang zhe na/_gang tshul khrims 
[\[264a.2\]]las sangs rgyas rjes su dran zhing zag pa med pa'i tshul khrims las kyang mi nyams pa'o/_/ting nge 'dzin las sangs rgyas rjes su dran zhing chos kyi dbyings mnyam pa thams cad las kyang mi 'gyur ba'o/_/shes rab las kyang sangs rgyas rjes su dran zhing chos thams cad rgyu ba 
[\[264a.3\]]med par yang rnam par mi rtog pa'o/_/rnam par grol ba las sangs rgyas rjes su dran zhing sems gnyis pa la yang mi gnas pa'o/_/rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba las sangs rgyas rjes su dran zhing shes pa thams cad la yang mi chags pa'o/_/stobs las sangs 
[\[264a.4\]]rgyas rjes su dran zhing dus gsum mtshungs pa'i ye shes las kyang mi g.yo ba'o/_/mi 'jigs pa las sangs rgyas rjes su dran zhing zag pa thams cad dang lhan cig tu yang mi gnas pa'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad las kyang sangs rgyas rjes su dran zhing chos kyi dbyings 
[\[264a.5\]]mnyam pa nyid du yang rnam par mi rtog pa'o/_/gzhan yang sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa ni gang gzugs las sangs rgyas yang dag par rjes su mi mthong la/_gzugs kyi ngo bo nyid shes pas dran pa'i ye shes kyi rjes su yang 'gro ba'o/_/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /_'du shes 
[\[264a.6\]]dang /_'du byed dang /_rnam par shes pa las sangs rgyas yang dag par rjes su mi mthong la/_rnam par shes pa'i ngo bo nyid shes pas dran pa'i ye shes kyi rjes su yang 'gro ba'o/_/phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched rnams las sangs rgyas yang dag par rjes su mi 
[\[264a.7\]]mthong la/_phung po dang khams dang skye mched kyi ngo bo nyid shes pas dran pa'i ye shes kyi rjes su yang 'gro ba'o/_/gang yang chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid shes pas shes rab che ba'i dran pa de ni yid la byed pa thams cad med de/_lta ba thams cad kun nas ldang ba med pa'o/_/
[\[264b\]]
[\[264b.1\]]gang de lta bu rnyog pa med pa'i dran pas dran pa nye bar gzhag pa shes pa de ni sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa zhes bya'o/_/gzhan yang sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa ni gang spyod lam thams cad du sangs rgyas la mngon par chags par mi byed do/_/'greng ba dang /_
[\[264b.2\]]'chag pa dang /_'dug pa dang /_nyal ba dang /_'gro ba dang /_'ong ba dang /_smra ba dang /_mi smra bar yang sangs rgyas la mngon par chags par mi byed do/_/de mngon par ma chags pas dran pa'ang dran par mi byed mi dran pa'ang dran par mi byed do/_/de dran pa yid la mi byed 
[\[264b.3\]]pas gzugs dang /_mtshan dang chos nyid du'ang sangs rgyas rjes su mi dran no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di ni sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa ste gang la mtshan ma dang dmigs pa thams cad kun du spyod pa med pa'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems 
[\[264b.4\]]dpa' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i chos rjes su dran pa gang zhe na/_chos zhes bya ba ni 'dod chags med pa'o/_/de la gang chos ci la chags kyang rung de dag la chos rjes su dran pa gang yang med do/_/chos zhes bya ba ni kun gzhi med pa'o/_/de la gang chos gang la 
[\[264b.5\]]sbyor ba de dag la chos rjes su dran pa gang yang med do/_/chos zhes bya ba ni nye bar zhi ba ste/_gang sems dang yid 'bar bar byed pa de dag la chos rjes su dran pa gang yang med do/_/chos zhes bya ba ni mtshan ma med pa ste/_gang mtshan ma'i rjes su 'brang ba'i rnam par 
[\[264b.6\]]shes pa de dag la chos rjes su dran pa gang yang med do/_/chos zhes bya ba ni 'dus ma byas pa ste/_gang 'dus byas su rab tu gzhag pa la gnas pa de dag la chos rjes su dran pa gang yang med do/_/yang chos rjes su dran pa ni gang gis 'du byed thams cad skye ba med par 
[\[264b.7\]]mthong ba ste/_yang dag pa nyid skyon med pa la 'jug pa'o/_/byang chub sems dpas chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis ma skyes par rig nas mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa rab tu 'thob bo/_/gang de lta bu'i gnas ni yang dag pa'i chos kyi gnas so/_/gang gis de ltar mngon 
[\[265a\]]
[\[265a.1\]]par rtogs pa de ni chos thams cad mngon par rtogs pa ste/_'di ni slob pa dang /_mi slob pa dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis mngon par rtogs pa'o/_/de ltar mnyam par gzhag pa ni 
[\[265a.2\]]'phags pa thams cad kyi rnam par grol ba ste/_de la chos gang yang kun du spyod pa med do/_/chos gang yang kun du spyod pa med pa de ni sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i chos rjes su dran pa'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i dge 'dun rjes su dran pa gang 
[\[265a.3\]]zhe na/_dge 'dun zhes bya ba ni 'dus ma byas pa ste/_mngon par 'du byed pas rjes su dran par mi nus so/_/gang na lus kyi las dang ngag gi las dang yid kyi las kun du spyod pa med pa de ni mngon par 'du mi byed pa zhes bya'o/_/de la 'dus byas zhes bya ba ni gdags pa dang tha snyad do/_/
[\[265a.4\]]'dus ma byas ni gdags pa thams cad yang dag par 'joms pa'o/_/tha snyad thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa ste/_rigs kyi bu 'di ni sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i dge 'dun rjes su dran pa'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i gtong ba 
[\[265a.5\]]rjes su dran pa gang zhe na/_gtong pa zhes bya ba ni phung po thams cad 'dor zhing rab tu gtong ba'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_des chos gang yang btang ba med do/_/'di ltar de la gang gtong ba'i chos gang yang snang bar mi 'gyur te/_'di la gang chos thams cad mi 'dzin pa de ni gtong 
[\[265a.6\]]ba'i mchog go/_/gang mi 'dzin pa de ni mi gtong ngo /_/gang mi gtong ba de ni mi snyems so/_/gang mi snyems pa de ni dmigs par mi byed do/_/dmigs par mi byed pa de la sems rgyu ba med do/_/rgyu ba med pa ni sems kyis rnam par rig pa med pa'o/_/gang la sems kyis 
[\[265a.7\]]rnam par rig pa med pa de ni rnam par shes pa mi gnas pa'o/_/rnam par shes pa mi gnas pa de ni sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i gtong ba rjes su dran pa'o/_/gang yang byang chub sems dpa'i gtong ba de ni thams cad mkhyen par bsngos pa ste/_gang thams cad mkhyen pa ni chos 
[\[265b\]]
[\[265b.1\]]thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid do/_/gang mnyam pa nyid la gnas pa ni rab tu gtong zhing byang chub tu 'ang bsngo ste/_de rjes su dran yang chos gang yang rjes su dran pa med do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_thams cad mkhyen pa ni chos dang chos ma yin pa gnyis dang ldan pa ma yin te/_gang 'di gnyis

[\[265b.3\]]pa gang zhe na/_tshul khrims zhes bya ba ni 'dus ma byas pa ste/_zag pa med pa/_smon lam med pa/_lhun gyis grub pa/_brtul zhugs grub pa/_brtul zhugs thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa ste/_rnam par rig pa'i mtshan nyid ma yin no/_/ting nge 'dzin gyis gnas te/_
[\[265b.4\]]sems kyi dmigs pa'i gnas ma yin no/_/shes rab kyi rtsa ba ste/_spros pa'i mtshan nyid ma yin no/_/rnam par grol ba'i rgyu 'thun pa ste/_gnyis kyis rab tu phye ba ma yin no/_/mkhas pas rab tu bsngags pa ste/_gzugs snang ba ma yin no/_/nyon mongs pa zhi ba 
[\[265b.5\]]ste/_so so rnam par rig pa'i mtshan nyid ma yin no/_/bde ba thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro zhing gnyen pos bskyed pa ma yin te gang de lta bu'i tshul khrims rjes su dran zhing dran pa la rnyog pa med pa de ni rigs kyi bu sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i tshul khrims rjes su dran pa zhes 
[\[265b.6\]]bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i lha rjes su dran pa gang zhe na/_lha'i ris gnyis rjes su dran par bya ste/_gnyis gang zhe na/_gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha dang skye ba gcig gis thogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas 
[\[265b.7\]]na 'khod pa rnams so/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha rnams rjes su dran no/_/yang skye ba gcig gis thogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na gang dag 'khod pa de dag ni spyi bo lta bu bcus rjes su dran te bcu gang zhe na/_pha 
[\[266a\]]
[\[266a.1\]]rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyi nang na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa spyi bor gyur pa dang /_mngon par shes pa thams cad kyi nang na mi nyams pa'i mngon par shes pa dang /_sa thams cad kyi nang na dbang bskur ba'i sa dang /_byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos thams 
[\[266a.2\]]cad kyi nang na yang dag pa'i lta ba dang ting nge 'dzin mi nyams pa dang /_so so yang dag par rig pa'i nang na don dang spobs pa rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /_shes pa thams cad kyi nang na chags pa med pa'i ye shes la 'jug pa dang /_dbang po thams cad kyi nang na rab 'bring tha ma la 
[\[266a.3\]]'jug pa shes pa dang /_stobs dang mi 'jigs pa la snang ba'i ye shes rjes su 'gro ba dang /_spyan thams cad kyi nang na sangs rgyas kyi spyan kun du gzigs pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad 'di dag gi lag mthil du song ba ltar 'dug pa dang /_byang chub kyi snying 
[\[266a.4\]]po la 'dug nas sems kyi skad cig gcig dang ldan pa'i shes rab kyis mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'o zhes spyi bo lta bu'i chos rnam pa bcu po de dag gis rjes su dran no/_/de'i dran pa de ni lta bas ma 'khrugs/_bag la nyal dang kun nas ldang bas ma 'khrugs/_yid la 
[\[266a.5\]]byed pas ma 'khrugs/_spros pas ma 'khrugs pa ste/_de lta bu'i dran pa rnyog pa med pas lha rjes su dran pa 'di ni rigs kyi bu sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba lha rjes su dran pa zhes bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mya ngan las 'das pa dang mnyam pa nyid 
[\[266a.6\]]thob kyang byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa yang spyod pa yin zhe na/_mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya ba ni nyon mongs pa thams cad rab tu zhi ba ste/_tshor ba thams cad rgyun bcad pa/_dmigs pa thams cad zhi ba/_phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched rnams kyi nying mtshams 
[\[266a.7\]]sbyor ba med pa ste/_skyes bu dam pa de mya ngan las 'das pa dang mnyam pa nyid thob cing /_sngon gnas bcas pa'i snying rje chen po dang /_thabs dang /_shes rab dang /_ye shes kyis ni rnam par rol/_sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis ni shin tu byin gyis brlabs/_shes rab dang /_
[\[266b\]]
[\[266b.1\]]ye shes ni shin tu bsgoms/_mngon gyi byin gyi rlabs kyis ni bsgom pa yongs su dag_/sgyu mas rnam par rol pa'i ting nge 'dzin la ni gnas te/_nyon mongs pa gang gis 'khor ba rab tu skyed pa'am/_'khor bar 'ching ba de dag rab tu spangs shing ye shes gang gis mya ngan las 
[\[266b.2\]]'das par ston pa de la dbang thob pas mi skye ba las skye zhing mi 'byung ba las 'byung ba ste/_mya ngan las 'das pa dang mnyam pa nyid du yang dag par zhugs kyang sems can yongs su smin par bya ba yang mi 'chad do/_/de ni rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi phrugs su 
[\[266b.3\]]tshad zhes bya ba'i ye shes kyi sgo ste/_ye shes kyi sgo gang la gnas pas byang chub sems dpa' mya ngan las 'das pa dang mnyam pa nyid thob kyang byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa'i 
[\[266b.4\]]mtshan nyid dang cho ga shes pa gang yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu sems can rnams kyi spyod pa ni brgyad khri bzhi stong ste/_'di ni sngon gyi rtsa ba'i tshig go/_/gang yang sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa brjod du med pa de sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyis 
[\[266b.5\]]mkhyen te/_nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyis mi rig_/byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes kyis mi rig pa de la byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs dang /_rang gi shes pas sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa'i mtshan nyid kyi rjes su rab tu 'jug ste/_
[\[266b.6\]]spyod pa de'i gang ngo bo nyid dam/_gang mtshan nyid kyang rung ste/_rgyu'i mtshan nyid dam/_rkyen gyi mtshan nyid dam/_byed rgyu'i mtshan nyid dam/_tshogs pa'i mtshan nyid dam/_tha dad pa'i mtshan nyid dam/_mi 'dra ba'i mtshan nyid dam/_'dod chags kyi mtshan nyid dam/_zhe 
[\[266b.7\]]sdang gi mtshan nyid dam/_gti mug gi mtshan nyid dam/_skal ba mnyam pa'i mtshan nyid dam/_sems can dmyal ba'i mtshan nyid dam/_dud 'gro'i skye gnas kyi mtshan nyid dam/_gshin rje'i 'jig rten gyi mtshan nyid dam/_lha'i mtshan nyid dam/_mi'i mtshan nyid dam/_
[\[267a\]]
[\[267a.1\]]nyan thos kyi nges par gyur pa'i mtshan nyid dam/_rang sangs rgyas kyi nges par gyur pa'i mtshan nyid dam/_sangs rgyas kyi nges par gyur pa'i mtshan nyid dam/_ring po'i rgyu'i mtshan nyid dam/_bar ma'i rgyu'i mtshan nyid dam/_nye ba'i rgyu'i mtshan nyid kyang rung ste/_
[\[267a.2\]]spyod pa de dag thams cad kyi mtshan nyid yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes te ye shes thams cad la kha na ma tho ba med pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa'i mtshan nyid dang cho ga shes pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la 
[\[267a.3\]]ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dag gi chos dkon mchog gi mdzod 'dzin pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dag gi chos dkon mchog gi mdzod ni mi zad pa ste/_sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa'i mtshan nyid la rab tu 'jug 
[\[267a.4\]]pa ji snyed pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos dkon mchog gi mdzod la gzud pa'i ye shes la 'jug pa'ang de snyed do/_/ji ltar sems can gyi spyod pa mi zad cing mtha' med pa de bzhin du sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos dkon mchog gi mdzod kyang mi

[\[272a.2\]]ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings te/_'di ni chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul la 'jug pa zhes bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu chos thams cad de ltar zhugs pas de la byang chub sems dpa' de ltar chos kyi tshul la zhugs pa'i gang ci smra ba dang brjod pa dang /_ston pa de thams cad ni chos 
[\[272a.3\]]kyi dbyings su yang dag par gzhol ba nyid do/_/chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul la zhugs pa de 'dod chags kyi khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag gnyis su med par rab tu shes so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'dod chags ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/zhe sdang gi 
[\[272a.4\]]khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_zhe sdang ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/gti mug gi khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gti mug ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/nyon 
[\[272a.5\]]mongs pa'i khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_nyon mongs pa ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/'dod pa'i khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'dod pa'i khams ni chos kyi dbyings 
[\[272a.6\]]kyi rang bzhin can no/_/gzugs kyi khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gzugs kyi khams ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/gzugs med pa'i khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_

[\[272b\]]
[\[272b.1\]]su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gzugs ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_mig gi rnam par shes pa ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/yid kyi 
[\[272b.2\]]bar gyi khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_yid kyi khams ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_yid kyi rnam 
[\[272b.3\]]par shes pa ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/phung po'i khams dang /_chos kyi dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_phung po'i khams ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/sa'i khams dang /_chu'i khams dang /_me'i khams dang /_

[\[272b.5\]]dbyings de dag ni gnyis su med de/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_chos thams cad ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can no/_/byang chub sems dpa' gang chos mnyam pa nyid 'di la zhugs pa de ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad la yang dag par gzhol ba'i phyir chos thams cad rab 
[\[272b.6\]]tu shes pa yin te/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi tshul la zhugs pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bsam pa rdo rje ltar sra zhing mi phyed de theg pa chen po 'di la legs par gnas shing mi g.yo ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi 
[\[272b.7\]]bu byang chub sems dpa' ni rdo rje ltar sra zhing mi phyed pa bcu gnyis kyis na lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis mi phyed pa yin te/_bcu gnyis gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_byang chub kyi sems mi phyed pa dang /_bsam pa dang /_lhag pa'i bsam pas sbyor ba mi phyed pa 
[\[273a\]]
[\[273a.1\]]dang /_sbyin pa mi phyed pa dang /_tshul khrims dang /_bzod pa dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_bsam gtan dang /_shes rab mi phyed pa dang /_byams pa chen po dang /_snying rje chen po mi phyed pa dang /_bsdu ba'i dngos po mi phyed pa dang /_sems can yongs 
[\[273a.2\]]su smin par byed pa mi phyed pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa mi phyed pa dang /_'khor bas yongs su mi skyo ba mi phyed pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa bas mi ngoms pa mi phyed pa dang /_mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po'i phyir gtan pa med pa'i mchod sbyin byed pa mi phyed pa 
[\[273a.3\]]dang /_dam pa'i chos yongs su gzung ba'i phyir lus dang srog 'dor bar mi phyed pa dang /_sems can gzhan dang gang zag gzhan gyi phyir dge ba'i rtsa ba bsngo ba mi phyed pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yang dag par sgrub pa mi phyed pa ste/_rigs kyi bu gang gi tshe byang 
[\[273a.4\]]chub sems dpa' chos 'di lta bu 'di dag las mi phyed pa de'i bsam pa rdo rje ltar sra zhing mi phyed pa yin no/_/'di lta ste dper na nor bu rin po che rdo rje ni nor bu rin po che thams cad 'bigs kyang bdag nyid ni mi phigs so/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' bsam pa 
[\[273a.5\]]rdo rje ltar sra zhing mi phyed pa yang sems can thams cad kyi bag la nyal dang /_nyon mongs pa kun nas ldang ba dang /_lta bar gyur pa rnams sel kyang bdag nyid ni theg pa chen po 'di las mi phyed do/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bsam pa rdo rje ltar 
[\[273a.6\]]sra zhing mi phyed de/_theg pa chen po 'di la legs par gnas shing mi g.yo ba yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi yul rjes su thob cing rang gi yul yongs su dag pa yin zhe na/_sangs rgyas kyi yul zhes bya ba ni yul ma 
[\[273a.7\]]yin pa/_yul med pa yul thams cad las yongs su dag pa'o/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' ni sangs rgyas kyi yul yongs su dag pas rang gi yul yongs su sbyong ste/_de 'di snyam du mig gi yul ni sangs rgyas kyi yul yin yang sangs rgyas kyi yul ni 
[\[273b\]]
[\[273b.1\]]su dang yang nye ba'am ring ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_sangs rgyas kyi yul ni dben pa'i yul yin pa'i phyir te/_dben pa'i yul la ni mig gi yul gang yang med do/_/rna ba'i yul ni sangs rgyas kyi yul yin yang /_sangs rgyas kyi yul ni su dang yang nye ba'am ring 
[\[273b.2\]]ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_sangs rgyas kyi yul ni dben pa'i yul yin pa'i phyir te/_dben pa'i yul la ni rna ba'i yul gang yang med do/_/sna'i yul ni sangs rgyas kyi yul yin yang sangs rgyas kyi yul ni su dang yang nye ba'am ring ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_
[\[273b.3\]]sangs rgyas kyi yul ni dben pa'i yul yin pa'i phyir te/_dben pa'i yul la ni sna'i yul gang yang med do/_/lce'i yul ni sangs rgyas kyi yul yin yang sangs rgyas kyi yul ni su dang yang nye ba'am ring ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_sangs rgyas kyi yul ni 
[\[273b.4\]]dben pa'i yul yin pa'i phyir te/_dben pa'i yul la ni lce'i yul gang yang med do/_/lus kyi yul ni sangs rgyas kyi yul yin yang /_sangs rgyas kyi yul ni su dang yang nye ba'am ring ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_sangs rgyas kyi yul ni dben pa'i yul 
[\[273b.5\]]yin pa'i phyir te/_dben pa'i yul la ni lus kyi yul gang yang med do/_/yid kyi yul ni sangs rgyas kyi yul yin yang sangs rgyas kyi yul ni su dang yang nye ba'am ring ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_sangs rgyas kyi yul ni dben pa'i yul yin pa'i phyir te dben 
[\[273b.6\]]pa'i yul la ni yid kyi yul gang yang med do/_/phung po rnams dang /_khams rnams dang /_skye mched rnams dang /_rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba yan lag bcu gnyis la yang de bzhin no/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi yul dben pa nyid 
[\[273b.7\]]kyi yul thams cad dben par 'jug go/_/rang gi zhing yongs su dag pas sangs rgyas kyi zhing la zhugs pa de ni chos gang dag de'i dbang po drug la snang bar gyur pa de dag thams cad la sangs rgyas kyi yul dben par 'jug go/_/ji ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul 
[\[274a\]]
[\[274a.1\]]chags pa med cing thogs pa med pa'i de bzhin du de yul thams cad la yang mi chags mi thogs te/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi yul la rjes su zhugs shing rang gi yul yongs su dag pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji 
[\[274a.2\]]ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gzungs thob pa rtag tu mi brjed par spyod pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' gzungs yongs su sbyang ba bya bas gzungs thob par gyur te/_de la gzungs yongs su sbyong ba byed pa gang zhe na/_rigs kyi bu sum cu rtsa gnyis 
[\[274a.3\]]po 'di dag ni gzungs yongs su sbyong ba byed pa ste/_sum cu rtsa gnyis po gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_chos don du gnyer ba dang /_chos 'dod pa dang /_chos kyi kun dga' la dga' mos pa dang /_chos la gzhol ba dang /_chos la bab pa dang /_mang du thos pa rnams la sten 
[\[274a.4\]]cing bsnyen la bsnyen bkur byed pa dang /_slob dpon dang mkhan po rnams la nga rgyal med cing btsun par gus par byed pa dang /_chos yongs su tshol bas mi ngoms pa dang /_rjes su bsten pa rnams la bka' blo bde zhing 'thun par 'dzin pa dang /_chos smra ba'i gang zag 
[\[274a.5\]]rnams la ston pa tsam du sems shing 'khrul pa mi tshol ba dang /_kun 'dzin pa dang /_klog pa dang /_ston pa la le lo med pa dang /_chos la slob dpon gyi dpe mkhyud med cing zang zing med par chos sbyin pa dang /_shes rab kyi rtsa ba'i gnas thos pa dang tshul 
[\[274a.6\]]bzhin gyi chos la sbyor ba dang /_ji ltar thos pa'i chos la so sor brtag pa'i phyir mang du thos pa mu med par byas pa dang /_tshangs par spyod pa la gnas pa mu med par byas pa dang /_rtag tu dben pa la sems gzhol ba dang /_rjes su dran pa drug rjes su dran pa dang /_
[\[274a.7\]]yang dag par chags par 'gyur ba drug mi 'dor ba dang /_gus par bya ba'i gnas drug yongs su mi gtong ba dang /_sems can thams cad la khong khro ba med pa'i sems kyis chos la sbyor ba dang /_rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba dang mi 'gal ba'i las dang /_bya ba 
[\[274b\]]
[\[274b.1\]]la 'jug pa dang /_rnam par thar pa'i sgo gsum la sems nges par rtog pa la mi skrag pa dang /_'phags pa'i rigs dang sbyangs pa'i yon tan kun 'dzin pas yong mi 'chad pa dang /_dam pa'i chos yongs su gzung ba la sems zhum pa med pa dang /_sems can la lta bas

[\[274b.5\]]pa shes pa dang /_bden pa la 'jug pa nges pa shes pa dang /_dran pa nye bar gzhag pa'i rjes su 'jug pa shes pa dang /_yang dag par spong ba'i brtson 'grus mi 'god pa shes pa dang /_rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pas rnam par rol pa shes pa dang /_dbang po'i rim pa shes 
[\[274b.6\]]pa dang /_stobs la gnas pa mi bskyod par shes pa dang /_byang chub kyi yan lag rtogs pa dang /_'thun pa shes pa dang /_lam la 'jug pa'i gnas shes pa dang /_zhi gnas kyis sems gnas par shes pa dang /_lhag mthong gis chos sgrol bar shes pa 
[\[274b.7\]]dang /_rig pa dang rnam par grol ba'i rjes su 'thun pa shes pa dang /_so so yang dag par rig pa la 'jug pa shes pa dang /_mngon par shes pa'i ye shes skyed pa shes pa dang /_pha rol tu phyin pa drug la 'jug pa shes pa dang /_bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi so so ci rigs

[\[276b.4\]]rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' skye ba dang 'chi 'pho dang 'byung ba rnams la dbang thob pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu chos bcu gnyis dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' skye ba dang 'chi 'pho ba dang 'byung ba rnams la dbang thob pa yin te/_bcu gnyis gang zhe na/_
[\[276b.5\]]'di lta ste/_sdig pa'i grogs po yongs su spangs shing rtag tu dge ba'i bshes gnyen la bsten pa dang /_lta ba shin tu yongs su dag pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i tshul khrims kyi phung po yongs su dag pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang snyoms par 'jug pa dang ldan pa shes pa dang /_
[\[276b.6\]]shes rab dang thabs zung du 'brel ba dang /_mngon par shes pa ma nyams pa thob pa dang /_ma skyes pa dang ma byung bar so sor rtog pa dang /_bsams bzhin du skye ba dang /_smon lam yongs su rdzogs pa dang /_sems can lta ba'i byams pa la sbyor ba dang /_snying rje chen po la nges par 
[\[276b.7\]]sems pa dang /_chos la sgyu ma lta bur rtog pa dang /_'chi 'pho med par 'pho zhing skye ba dang /_chos kyi mtshan nyid rmi lam gyi ngo bo nyid kyis chos la 'jug pa dang /_phyin ci log gi chos la yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rtog pa dang /_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi byin gyis brlabs 
[\[277a\]]
[\[277a.1\]]pa nas skye ste/_'pho zhing 'byung la skye ba dang /_'pho ba dang /_'byung bar yang mi sems pa'o/_/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' gang dang gang du skyes nas sems can gyi don byed pa'i skyes pa'i rabs bstan pa tshad med grangs med pa'i skyes pa'i rabs de dag thams cad ni 
[\[277a.2\]]rtsa ba gnyis la gnas pa ste/_'di lta ste/_mngon par shes pa'i ye shes la gnas pa dang /_snying rje chen po'i rtsa ba la gnas pa'o/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' skye ba dang /_'chi 'pho dang 'byung ba rnams la dbang thob pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na 
[\[277a.3\]]byang chub sems dpa' bdud bzhi'i lam las yang dag par 'das shing bdud dang phyir rgol ba thams cad yang dag par bcom pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad sgyu ma lta bur shes shing rtogs pas phung po'i bdud las yang dag par 'da'o/_/
[\[277a.4\]]ji ltar thos pa'i chos la tshul bzhin so sor rtogs pas nyon mongs pa'i bdud las yang dag par 'da'o/_/rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba rtogs pas 'chi bdag gi bdud las yang dag par 'da'o/_/byang chub kyi sems mi nyams pas lha'i bu'i bdud las yang dag par 'da'o/_/yang 
[\[277a.5\]]des bdud kyi las rnams bcom pa yin te/_des bdud kyi las rnams bcom pa gang zhe na/_gang dag gis byang chub sems dpa' la bdud kyis glags mi rnyed pa ste/_theg pa dman pa 'dod pa bdud kyi las dang /_byang chub kyi sems mi srung ba bdud kyi las dang /_sems can tha 
[\[277a.6\]]dad par sbyin pa bdud kyi las dang /_skye ba la gnas pa'i tshul khrims bdud kyi las dang /_de bzhin du sbyar te/_gzugs su bsngos pa'i bzod pa dang /_'jig rten gyi bya ba la brtson pa'i brtson 'grus dang /_de la chags shing ro myong ba'i bsam gtan dang /_shes rab kyi chos ngan pa 
[\[277a.7\]]la smod pa bdud kyi las dang /_'khor bas yongs su skyo ba bdud kyi las dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su mi sngo ba bdud kyi las dang /_nyon mongs pa la mi smod pa bdud kyi las dang /_ltung ba 'chab pa bdud kyi las dang /_byang chub sems dpa' la sdang ba bdud kyi las dang /_
[\[277b\]]
[\[277b.1\]]dam pa'i chos spong ba bdud kyi las dang /_byams pa rgyun gcod pa bdud kyi las dang /_pha rol tu phyin pa rnams yongs su mi tshol ba bdud kyi las dang /_dam pa'i chos mnyan par mi 'dod pa bdud kyi las dang /_chos la slob dpon gyi dpe mkhyud byed pa bdud kyi las dang /_
[\[277b.2\]]zang zing dang 'brel par chos ston pa bdud kyi las dang /_thabs dang bral bas sems can yongs su smin par byed pa bdud kyi las dang /_bsdu ba'i dngos po 'dor ba bdud kyi las dang /_tshul khrims 'chal pa rnams la khong khro ba bdud kyi las dang /_tshul khrims dang ldan 
[\[277b.3\]]pa rnams la mi gus pa bdud kyi las dang /_nyan thos kyi bslab pa'i rjes su 'thun pa bdud kyi las dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa can gyi rjes su 'jug pa bdud kyi las dang /_dus ma yin par bsgrub pa 'dod pa bdud kyi las dang /_snying rje chen po bor nas rigs la rtog pa bdud 
[\[277b.4\]]kyi las dang /_'dus ma byas 'dod pa bdud kyi las dang /_'dus byas la smod pa bdud kyi las dang /_sems sems can gyi ci bya'o cog la g.yel ba bdud kyi las dang /_nga rgyal la gnas shing mi 'dud pa dang /_lce gnyis smra ba dang /_g.yo sgyu dang ldan pa dang /_bcos ma la dga' ba 
[\[277b.5\]]dang /_mi drang ba dang /_rtsub cing brlang ba dang /_sdig pa la mi smod pa dang /_chos kyi drungs 'byin pa dang /_thos pa cung zad tsam gyis chog par 'dzin pa dang /_chos ma yin pa don du gnyer ba dang /_chos ma yin pa 'dod pa dang /_sgrib pa dang /_chod pa dang /_kun nas ldang ba rnam par mi 
[\[277b.6\]]gnon pa bdud kyi las dang /_sems kyi dri ma mi rtog pa bdud kyi las dang /_dge sbyong gi dri ma la mi mjed pa bdud kyi las te/_rigs kyi bu ji tsam du mi dge ba'i chos la 'jug pa dang /_dge ba'i chos 'dor ba de thams cad ni bdud kyi las so/_/chos bzhis ni bdud kyi las 'di 
[\[277b.7\]]dag las shin tu 'da' ste/_bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_byang chub kyi sems ma brjed pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa drug po dag dang /_dad pa dang brtson 'grus la gnas pa'i bag yod pa dang thabs la mkhas pa la gnas te sems can yongs su smin par byed pa dang /_chos kyi tshul zab mo la gnas 
[\[278a\]]
[\[278a.1\]]te dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pas so/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar chos la sbyor ba'i byang chub sems dpa' ni bdud bzhi'i lam las yang dag par 'das shing bdud dang phyir rgol ba thams cad bcom pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[278a.2\]]bsod nams tshad med pa bsags shing sems can thams cad 'tsho bar gyur pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' ni sems can rnams la byams pas gnas pa yin/_'byung po rnams la snying rje bar gnas pa yin/_bsam gtan rnams la de la ma chags 
[\[278a.3\]]par gnas pa yin/_slong ba rnams la bdog pa thams cad yongs su gtong ba yin/_bsod nams dag gis longs spyod mi zad pa yin/_bsam pa dag pas lag na rin po che yin/_mthon dman gyi yid med pas sems sa dang mtshungs pa yin/_tshul khrims yongs su dag 
[\[278a.4\]]pas bsam pa 'byor ba yin/_mchod sbyin thams cad sgrub pas sems grol zhing sgo bsgrib pa med pa yin/_chos rnam par 'byed pas gzungs dang spobs pa phun sum tshogs pa yin/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing tshad med par 'gro bas mngon par shes pa'i ye shes la gnas pa 
[\[278a.5\]]yin no/_/ji ltar phyi rol gyi 'byung ba chen po bzhi po 'di dag sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa de bzhin du bdag gi nang gi 'byung ba chen po bzhi po 'di dag kyang sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur cig_/bdag gi dge ba'i rtsa ba dang /_chos shes pa la mkhas pa 
[\[278a.6\]]gang yin pa de sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho ba ma yin par ma gyur cig ces de ltar dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams kyang yongs su bsngo ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bsod nams tshad med pa bsags shing sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 
[\[278a.7\]]de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas rnams ma byung yang sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pas sems can rnams la nye bar gnas pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' ni gnas dang gnas ma yin pa'i bya ba shes pas stobs bcu yongs su sbyang bar bya ba tshar 
[\[278b\]]
[\[278b.1\]]phyin pa yin/_/zag pa zad pa'i bya ba shes pas mi 'jigs pa bzhi yongs su sbyang bar bya ba tshar phyin pa yin/_dus gsum la chags pa med pa'i ye shes kyi bya ba shes pas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad yongs su sbyang bar bya ba tshar phyin pa yin/_sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyi snang ba thob pas

[\[278b.3\]]nams kyi tshogs thams cad bsags pas lus dang /_ngag dang /_sems dang /_mtshan dang /_dpe byad bzang po'i rgyan yongs su sbyang bar bya ba tshar phyin pa yin/_dbang bskur ba thob pas sa bcu yongs su sbyang bar bya ba tshar phyin pa yin/_ye shes kyi tshogs bsags pas sangs rgyas

[\[278b.6\]]de'i phyir rgya mtsho chen po zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin du sems can thams cad kyi sems su snang ba'am/_gzugs su snang ba'am/_sgrar snang ba ji snyed pa de dag thams cad rgya mtsho'i phyag rgya'i ting nge 'dzin la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' snang ba gcig la snang bar 'gyur te/_de'i

[\[279a\]]
[\[279a.1\]]bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang dus dang dus ma yin pa la so sor rtog pas byang chub kyi snying po'i dus las mi yol lo/_/'di lta ste/_dper na rgya mtsho chen po ni shi ba'i ro dang lhan cig mi gnas so/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang bag chags kyi nyon mongs pa thams

[\[279b\]]
[\[279b.1\]]bral zhing snang ba thob pa yang yin te de la mun pa zhes bya ba ni gang chos rnams la mi shes pa'o/_/snang ba zhes bya ba ni gang chos rnams la ye shes thogs pa med pa ste/_de'i phyir de dag mun pa dang bral zhing snang ba thob pa zhes bya'o/_/skyes bu dam pa de dag ni ye shes gzhan gyi dring la mi 'jog cing rang gis

[\[279b.3\]]par gyur pa de'i tshe myur du thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes thob par 'gyur ro/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/gang zhig lta ba kun las sems dben zhing /_/mkhas pa bsod nams tshogs ni rab byed la/_/mtshan ma ldang ba yongs su spangs

[\[279b.5\]]dang mtshan gyi chos su ma yin te/_/gang na sems dang yid ni mi rgyu ba/_/de ni 'thun par sangs rgyas rjes su dran/_/chos ni 'dod chags bral zhing rang bzhin dben/_/zhi zhing rab zhi rtag tu mtshan ma med/_/gang zhig dmigs pa thams cad bral gyur pa/_/de 'dir chos ni rjes su dran pa 
[\[279b.6\]]yin/_/'phags pa'i dge 'dun shin tu 'dus ma byas/_/stong pa mtshan med smon lam rnam par grol/_/de la sems med dran dang 'jug pa med/_/de ni dge 'dun rjes su dran pa yin/_/phung po yongs su btang ba thams cad gtong /_/nyon mongs dngos po thams cad legs par

[\[280a\]]

[\[280b\]]
[\[280b.1\]]sde stong phrag bye ba rab du 'chad/_/sems can dbang por sems can 'du shes med/_/sangs rgyas mthu yis bskal pa bye stong du/_/rgyun mi 'chad par chos ni rab ston cing /_/bsam pa ji bzhin sems can tshim par byed/_/spobs pa'i tshul gyis sangs rgyas yon tan gnas/_/chos 'di

[\[280b.3\]]gsal ba/_/de ni bstan pa 'di la bag yod yin/_/phung po yang dag sgyu ma lta bur mthong /_/chos dbyings ji 'dra de bzhin khams rnams shes/_/dbang po drug ni grong stong 'dra mthong ba/_/de ni phung po'i bdud las yang dag 'da'/_/sprin brtsegs phung po lta bur nyon mongs rnams/_/bag

[\[280b.6\]]min/_/'gro ba'i sems ni sgyu ma 'drar mthong ba/_/de ni sems dpa'i rdzi ba med pa yin/_/pha rol tshu rol gang la'ang mi chags la/_/chos ldan gru ni yang dag su sbyor zhing /_/sems can sgrol yang sems can 'du shes med/_/mnyam pa zhes bya byang chub sems dpa' de/_/srid pa gsum 
[\[280b.7\]]po 'brog dgon 'dra ba dang /_/stong par 'gyur ba med par rab shes shing /_/lam gyi rim gyis rtag tu sems can sgrol/_/de ni ded dpon bdud rtsi lam ston pa/_mdzangs pa de ni tshul dang tshul min mkhas/_/chos rnams gdod nas stong par rab tu shes/_/tshul dang rigs pas mya ngan 'das la 'dzud/_/
[\[281a\]]
[\[281a.1\]]byang chub sems dpa' de ni 'dren ces bya/_/sems kyi rgyud ni gzhan dang gzhan du 'gyur/_/sems ni sems la nams kyang mtshams mi sbyor/_/'gro ba'i sems rgyud de dag rab shes pa/_/byang chub sems dpa' yongs su 'dren ces bya/_/nam mkha'i ngo bo nyid dang chu zla ltar/_/chos 
[\[281a.2\]]rnams thams cad dag cing rnam dag ces/_/nyon mongs kun dang 'dre bar mi byed pa/_/sems can dag pa de ni ma smad pa/_/gang zhig chos gcig gis ni chos rnams kun/_/sgyu ma smig rgyu 'dra bar gzung med dang /_/gsog dang [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]dang rtag pa med par shes/_/de ni byang chub snying por 
[\[281a.3\]]myur du 'gro/_/chos gtan la 'bebs pa bstan pa 'di bshad pa na/_lha dang /_mi dang /_lha ma yin dang /_mi'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po'i srog chags khrag khrig phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed do/_/byang chub sems dpa' sum khri nyis stong ni

[\[290a.6\]]ces smras so/_/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin byang chub kyi snying por thogs pa med par 'gro ba bsam pa dag pa zhes bya ba yod do/_/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin sems can thams cad la khong khro ba med pa'i 'od zer ces bya ba

[\[290b.1\]]bar 'gyur ro/_/dge ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin ni rnyog pa med par 'gyur ro/_/mngon par 'phags pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin ni spyi gtsug bltar mi mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/rnam par sel ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin ni nyon mongs pa thams cad 'dul bar 'gyur ro/_/'khyil pa zhes

[\[300a.1\]]de ni gzugs kyis de bzhin nyid rjes su shes pa ste/_gzugs 'jig pa ma yin/_chad pa ma yin/_rtag pa ma yin/_rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba rang bzhin gyis ma skyes pa'o/_/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /_'du shes dang /_'du byed rnams dang /_rnam par shes
